			hidden_effect = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							any_province = {
							country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 	
								
									OR = {
									continent = europe
								}
							}
							
							}
							set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_europe
						}
					if = {
						limit = {
							any_province = {
							country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 	

								
									OR = {
									continent = africa
								}
							
							}
							}
							set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_africa
						}

				limit = {
					any_province = {
							country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 	

						
							OR = {
							continent = asia
						}
					
					}
					}

				}

							country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 	

						
							OR = {
							continent = north_america
						}
					
					}
					}
					set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_namerica
				}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_province = {

						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 	
					
						OR = {
						continent = south_america
					}
				
				}
				}
				set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_samerica
			}																						
				}
				}

	option = {

		immediate = {
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_province = {
								country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 	

								OR = {
								superregion = europe_superregion
									}
							}
							}
					set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_west_europe
					}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_province = {
								country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 	

								OR = {
								superregion = eastern_europe_superregion
									}
							}
							}
							set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_east_europe
						}																						
				}
				}

	option = {
		name = cosmo_eumarriage_events.21.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
				capital_scope = {
					superregion = europe_superregion
				}
				has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_west_europe
				}
				country_event = {
					id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.210
					}
			}
		if = {
			limit = {
				capital_scope = {
					superregion = europe_superregion
				}
			NOT = {
				has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_west_europe
			}
			has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_east_europe					
				}

					}
			}
		if = {

					}
			}
		if = {
			limit = {
				capital_scope = {
					superregion = eastern_europe_superregion
				}
			NOT = {
				has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_east_europe
			}
			has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_west_europe					
				}
				country_event = {
					id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.210
					}
				}
		if = {
			limit = {
				capital_scope = {
					NOT = {
						continent = europe
					}
				}
				has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_east_europe
				}

					id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.211
					}

					}
					country_event = {

						}
				}																	
	}

				superregion = china_superregion

				superregion = far_east_superregion
				superregion = oceania_superregion
				}
				}
			NOT = {
				has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_east_asia
			}
			has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_west_asia					
				}
				country_event = {
					id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.230
					}
				}
		if = {
			limit = {
				capital_scope = {
					NOT = {
						continent = asia
					}
				}
				has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_east_asia
				}
				country_event = {
					id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.231
					}
			}
				if = {
					limit = {
					capital_scope = {
					NOT = {
						continent = asia
					}	
					}
				NOT = {
					has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_east_asia
				}
				has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_west_asia					
					}
					country_event = {
						id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.230
						}
				}																	
	}
		
}

					has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_africa
					has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_europe

					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.24
				}
			}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_south_america
					has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_africa
					has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_europe
				}				
				OR = {
					has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_namerica					
					has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_possible_samerica					
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.24
				}
			}																									
	}		
}

	goto = honeymoonprovince
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		has_consort = yes 
		has_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		has_country_modifier = cosmo_on_honeymoon
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = honeymoonprovince
				}
				random_list = {
				25 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_consort_bonus
				}
				25 = { #dip
					trigger = {
					NOT = {
						has_ruler_flag = has_honeymoon_monarchpoints
					}						
					}
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_ruler_bonus
	
				}
				25 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_province_bonus
				}
				25 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_fertility_bonus
				}																								
			}
				random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3
				}																								
			}								
	}
	}

		option = {#launch 2nd
		name = cosmo_eumarriage_events.1001.a
		trigger = {
			has_consort = yes		
				}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_global_flag = cosmo_buildings_active
			}
			custom_tooltip = royal_visit_building_hmoonactive_tt
		}		
		event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = honeymoon_visit
				duration = 90 
				desc = honeymoon_visit_DESC
	
			}
		}										
				trigger_switch = {
					on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
					honeymoon_consort_bonus = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = zealot_personality  
						add_queen_personality = tolerant_personality 
						}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = babbling_buffoon_personality  
						remove_queen_personality = naive_personality  
						add_queen_personality = silver_tongue_personality
						}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = infertile_personality  
						add_queen_personality = fertile_personality
						}										
					}
					honeymoon_ruler_bonus = {
						set_ruler_flag = has_honeymoon_monarchpoints
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							controller = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							} 	
						}	
							}
							change_dip = 1				
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							owned_by = ROOT 	
						}	
							}
							change_adm = 1				
						}																		
						}
					honeymoon_province_bonus = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							controller = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							} 	
						}	
							}
						event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							add_scaled_local_dip_power = 1 	
						}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							owned_by = ROOT 	
						}	
							}
						event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							add_scaled_local_adm_power = 1 	
						}
						}																
						}
					honeymoon_fertility_bonus = {
						add_ruler_modifier = {
							name = cosmo_fertile_ruler
							duration = -1  
							desc = cosmo_fertile_ruler_DESC
							hidden = no				
						}
					}																
				}	
			country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.1002
			    days = 30
				random = 15
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_consort_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_ruler_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_province_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3
			}					
}		
		option = {#end early
		name = cosmo_eumarriage_events.1001.b
		remove_country_modifier = cosmo_on_honeymoon 
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_consort_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_ruler_bonus

				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3

					limit = {

							has_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
						}
					}

					has_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
					has_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}

				}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
				NOT = {
					has_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
				}	
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
				}
					save_event_target_as = honeymoonprovince
				}				
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
				NOT = {
					has_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
				}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
					development = 12
				}
					save_event_target_as = honeymoonprovince
				}				
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
				NOT = {
					has_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
				}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
					province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1					
				}
					save_event_target_as = honeymoonprovince
				}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
				NOT = {
					has_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
				}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
 									
				}
					save_event_target_as = honeymoonprovince
				}
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
					has_province_flag = honeymoon_chosen_here
				NOT = {
					has_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
				}
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
 									
				}
					save_event_target_as = honeymoonprovince
				}								
				random_list = {
				25 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_consort_bonus
				}
				25 = { #dip
					trigger = {
					NOT = {
						has_ruler_flag = has_honeymoon_monarchpoints
					}						
					}
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_ruler_bonus
	
				}
				25 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_province_bonus
				}
				25 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_fertility_bonus
				}																								
			}
				random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3
				}																								
			}								
	}
	}

		option = {#launch third
		name = cosmo_eumarriage_events.1002.a
		trigger = {
			has_consort = yes		
				}
		custom_tooltip = royal_visit_building_hmoonactive_tt
		event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
			clr_province_flag = honeymoon_chosen_here
			set_province_flag = honeymoon_second_stop
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = honeymoon_visit
				duration = 90 
				desc = honeymoon_visit_DESC
	
			}	
		}						
				trigger_switch = {
					on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
					honeymoon_consort_bonus = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = zealot_personality  
						add_queen_personality = tolerant_personality 
						}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = babbling_buffoon_personality  
						remove_queen_personality = naive_personality  
						add_queen_personality = silver_tongue_personality
						}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = infertile_personality  
						add_queen_personality = fertile_personality
						}										
					}
					honeymoon_ruler_bonus = {
						set_ruler_flag = has_honeymoon_monarchpoints
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							controller = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							} 	
						}	
							}
							change_dip = 1				
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							owned_by = ROOT 	
						}	
							}
							change_adm = 1				
						}																		
						}
					honeymoon_province_bonus = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							controller = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							} 	
						}	
							}
						event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							add_scaled_local_dip_power = 1 	
						}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							owned_by = ROOT 	
						}	
							}
						event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							add_scaled_local_adm_power = 1 	
						}
						}																
						}
					honeymoon_fertility_bonus = {
						add_ruler_modifier = {
							name = cosmo_fertile_ruler
							duration = -1  
							desc = cosmo_fertile_ruler_DESC
							hidden = no				
						}
					}																
				}	
			country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.1003
			    days = 30
				random = 15
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_consort_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_ruler_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_province_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3
			}					
}		
		option = {#end early
		name = cosmo_eumarriage_events.1002.b
		remove_country_modifier = cosmo_on_honeymoon 
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_consort_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_ruler_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_province_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3
				every_province = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_second_stop
						}
					}
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_chosen_here
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_second_stop
				}
			}							
}

				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3
			}					
}		
		option = {#end early
		name = cosmo_eumarriage_events.1003.b
		remove_country_modifier = cosmo_on_honeymoon 		
		event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
			clr_province_flag = honeymoon_chosen_here
			set_province_flag = honeymoon_third_stop
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = honeymoon_visit
				duration = 90 
				desc = honeymoon_visit_DESC
	
			}	
		}
				trigger_switch = {
					on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
					honeymoon_consort_bonus = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = zealot_personality  
						add_queen_personality = tolerant_personality 
						}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = babbling_buffoon_personality  
						remove_queen_personality = naive_personality  
						add_queen_personality = silver_tongue_personality
						}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = infertile_personality  
						add_queen_personality = fertile_personality
						}										
					}
					honeymoon_ruler_bonus = {
						set_ruler_flag = has_honeymoon_monarchpoints
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							controller = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							} 	
						}	
							}
							change_dip = 1				
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							owned_by = ROOT 	
						}	
							}
							change_adm = 1				
						}																		
						}
					honeymoon_province_bonus = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							controller = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							} 	
						}	
							}
						event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							add_scaled_local_dip_power = 1 	
						}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							owned_by = ROOT 	
						}	
							}
						event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							add_scaled_local_adm_power = 1 	
						}
						}																
						}
					honeymoon_fertility_bonus = {
						add_ruler_modifier = {
							name = cosmo_fertile_ruler
							duration = -1  
							desc = cosmo_fertile_ruler_DESC
							hidden = no				
						}
					}																
				}				
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_consort_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_ruler_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_province_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3
				every_province = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_second_stop
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_third_stop
						}
					}
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_chosen_here
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_second_stop
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_third_stop
				}
			}							
}	
}

						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_province_bonus
				}
				25 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_fertility_bonus
				}																								
			}
				random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3
				}																								
			}								
	}
	}

		option = {#launch third
		name = cosmo_eumarriage_events.1005.a
		trigger = {
			has_consort = yes
			OR = {
				has_consort_flag = cosmo_europe_marriage_19	
			}
				}
		event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
			set_province_flag = honeymoon_fifth_stop
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = honeymoon_visit
				duration = 90 
				desc = honeymoon_visit_DESC
	
			}	
		}						
				trigger_switch = {
					on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
					honeymoon_consort_bonus = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = zealot_personality  
						add_queen_personality = tolerant_personality 
						}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = babbling_buffoon_personality  
						remove_queen_personality = naive_personality  
						add_queen_personality = silver_tongue_personality
						}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3	
						}
						remove_queen_personality = infertile_personality  
						add_queen_personality = fertile_personality
						}										
					}
					honeymoon_ruler_bonus = {
						set_ruler_flag = has_honeymoon_monarchpoints
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							controller = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							} 	
						}	
							}
							change_dip = 1				
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							owned_by = ROOT 	
						}	
							}
							change_adm = 1				
						}																		
						}
					honeymoon_province_bonus = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							controller = {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							} 	
						}	
							}
						event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							add_scaled_local_dip_power = 1 	
						}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
							event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							owned_by = ROOT 	
						}	
							}
						event_target:honeymoonprovince = {
							add_scaled_local_adm_power = 1 	
						}
						}																
						}
					honeymoon_fertility_bonus = {
						add_ruler_modifier = {
							name = cosmo_fertile_ruler
							duration = -1  
							desc = cosmo_fertile_ruler_DESC
							hidden = no				
						}
					}																
				}	
			country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_eumarriage_events.1006
			    days = 30
				random = 15
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_consort_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_ruler_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_province_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3
			}					
}		
		option = {#end early
		name = cosmo_eumarriage_events.1005.b
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_consort_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_ruler_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_province_bonus
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_1
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_2
				clr_ruler_flag = honeymoon_bonus_3
				every_province = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_second_stop
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_third_stop
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_fourth_stop
							has_province_flag = honeymoon_fifth_stop
						}
					}
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_possible_here
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_first_stop
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_second_stop
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_third_stop
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_fourth_stop
					clr_province_flag = honeymoon_fifth_stop
				}
			}							
}
